| `ui/css/bag.css` | Styles for the Bag tab: Purse and load, Assets, item grid and sheets; tokens, plus the mock's fixed tints and rarity borders in both themes, layout follows the container. Every rule is scoped to .bag-tab or .bag-sheet so no other tab is touched. | — | — | — | — |
| `ui/css/base.css` | Base styles: reset, font and focus rules for everything inside the suite root. | — | — | — | — |
| `ui/css/card.css` | Character card styles (decisions 19-21): tokens renamed from --g-* to --su-*. | — | — | — | — |
| `ui/css/chat.css` | Inline row and people drawer: SillyTavern theme variables only, never the suite tokens (decision 34). | — | — | — | — |
| `ui/css/dossier.css` | Styles for the dossier: top bar, hero ring, summary cards, pager and the page bodies; tokens plus the mock's sky and fixed colours, sized from the container. | — | — | — | — |
| `ui/css/fight.css` | Styles for the Fight tab: header, foes, party, action tiles, log and end card; tokens only, sized from the container. | — | — | — | — |
| `ui/css/journal.css` | Styles for the Journal tab: quest cards, leads, closed list, calendar, day panel, coming up and the edit form; tokens, plus the mock's fixed tints and text colours in both themes. | — | — | — | — |
| `ui/css/kit.css` | Kit styles: the one button, chip, switch and segmented-control family, sized for touch. | — | — | — | — |
| `ui/css/next-hide.css` | Hides the old Story Suite furniture in the chat only while the body carries ss-ui-next. | — | — | — | — |
| `ui/css/now.css` | Styles for the Now tab cards, the Show-all list, the spoiler switch and the feelings ring; tokens, plus the mock's fixed late, fight, torn, ring and tracker-bar tones and its per-scheme feeling colours. | — | — | — | — |
| `ui/css/party.css` | Party tab styles (decision 22, party.html): member row, sheet, equipment doll, stats, skills and the bottom sheets; tokens, plus the mock's fixed stance, rarity, mastery and scrim colours in both themes. | — | — | — | — |

## Feature entry points

| Feature | Start reading at | What it covers |
|---|---|---|
| Runtime start-up | `core/runtime.js` | Builds the host, registers modules and hooks chat events. |
| Prompt building | `core/prompt.js` | Assembles the static text and tail Story Block adds to each reply's prompt. |
| Reply capture | `core/capture.js` | Reads state changes out of a finished reply. |
| Saving and persistence | `core/persist.js` | Stores story state in the chat and its files. |
| Replay and rewind | `core/replay.js` | Rebuilds state from the chat after a swipe, edit or delete. |
| Commands | `core/commands.js` | The command registry every screen and module changes state through. |
| Module registry | `core/registry.js` | registerModule: how modules add parts, screens, widgets and queries. |
| Public API | `core/public-api.js` | The frozen read-only StoryBlock.api other extensions use. |
| Model calls | `core/model-call.js` | Helper model calls and their connection profile. |
